## 11. Worked examples (full procedure, expected outputs)

**R10 reading:** the older rows below retain their cited calibration evidence. Their secondary-job lists/order must be re-derived by §6.10 before reuse as current expected outputs; they are not completed r11 results. The three R10 worked cases in §6.10 illustrate the current signal procedure.

All twenty-one are real records; ranks re-read from the raw INCI with lexicon v0.1 (node), §11.13 from the frozen round-2 cohort, §11.14–§11.16 from the round-2 cohort plus the re-check capture `capture/recheck-r6.json` (2026-10-12), and §11.17–§11.21 from the frozen round-3 cohort `r3/cohort.json` read under v0.4 / lexicon v0.4 (node, 2026-10-13). §11.14–§11.16 state the S0 reading the R2-H rulings fix; §11.17–§11.21 state the reading the round-3 rulings fix — their full profiles are derived blind in the confirmation round 4. They are named in this standard, so in calibration they are **confirmation-grade only** (§10.2). Where a value rests on a Phase 2 ruling, the cell cites its PH2 ID.

Cross-check without PH2-1: 3.4 (iii) LI G0 — PVP r3 (FX-1) above T_LI, no conditioning ⇒ `excluded_styling_first` with C2 hold positioning ⇒ `styling`. Not held (LI L5 sees PVP; Leave-in excludes it) |

| Hold | Taft gel scale is not comparable (4–14) ⇒ words: „maximalen … Halt" (C2), name „POWER" ⇒ **4**, confidence high. D8b: no flag. Neutral fact: n/a (level stated) |

### 11.2 Balea Professional Styling Cream Traumlocken — `balea-professional-styling-cream-traumlocken`

| Step | Output |
|---|---|
| Identity | house-brand C2 (dm ↔ Balea, LI rule 6) ⇒ `house_brand_ownership`; INCI dm.de; directions house-brand C2 |
| Markers | T_S Sodium Benzoate r10, plausible · T_LI Sodium Benzoate r10 |
| S0 | class: v0.2 read **`mixed`** (definition + „Verbesserte Geschmeidigkeit" + „Hitzeschutz bis zu 220 °C", round-2 G0 lane) ⇒ PH1-12: D7 first. **R2-H5:** „Verbesserte Geschmeidigkeit" (a dm bullet; the pack says „Locken-Forming-System für Geschmeidigkeit und verbesserte Kämmbarkeit.") is a feel line and never the care half, and the heat line is a bullet, so the class reads `styling_result` (`li_function: yes`) and 3.4 (i) runs the same D7 test; whichever class a lane reads, D7 runs before the mixed stop (PH1-12) and the product keeps `curl_cream`. The round-3 freeze adds the C1 pack lines („BIS ZU 24h DEFINIERTE LOCKEN" — a duration; „Perfekt definierte, glänzende Locken und Hitzeschutz bis 220 °C"; „PFLEGE-FORMEL MIT SHEABUTTER UND KERATIN" — an ingredient call-out, SFS-15; „SCHWUNGVOLL. DEFINIERT. VERWÖHNT."); none changes this path. D7 — VP/VA Copolymer r3 above T_LI ✓; substantive conditioning (LI COND high: LGN pair Cetearyl Alcohol r2 + Cetrimonium Chloride r9, esters r4–r6, Polyquaternium-37 r8) ✓; sold as „Styling Cream Traumlocken" ✓ ⇒ **`styling` `curl_cream`**. PH1-16: the locked LI G0 letter reads `provisional_boundary` (G0 lane, LI §2.3.2 clause 2) ⇒ Leave-in could claim it ⇒ **`provisional_boundary (pending_leave_in_addendum)`, lean `styling`/`curl_cream`** |
| F | mechanisms `fixative_film` [VP/VA r3] + `wax_lipid` [Isopropyl Palmitate r4, Dicaprylyl Carbonate r5, Caprylic/Capric Triglyceride r6] · hold_route `present` · weight **`rich`** (LGN pair; families a, c, e) — the property-set dry run said `medium` because it omitted LI's LGN row (§6.3) · buildup **fires** (limb 1: Polyquaternium-37 r8; limb 2: rich) · alcohol_note no · flags silicones (Dimethicone r12, tail), oils (Shea r17, tail), fragrance; candidate_below_tail: Dimethicone r12/Trimethylsiloxyphenyl Dimethicone r13 would add `silicone_coat` ⇒ review |

## 12. Ruled (PH2-1…PH2-6)

The six proposals of the 2026-10-10 draft (P-S1…P-S6) were ruled by Nick on 2026-10-11 as PH2-1…PH2-6 (`plans/styling-concept/02-rulings.md`, "Phase 2 research-engine rulings"). None is open; the body of this standard states each ruled rule. Six further rulings (PH2-7…PH2-12) closed ledger ambiguities (§13).

| Ruling | Former proposal | What was ruled | Where it lives | Real example |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| **PH2-1** | P-S1 | The Leave-in border check applies **only where the promise overlaps Leave-in's own definition** (care, detangling, smoothing/anti-frizz, curl support, blow-dry/heat support; LI §2.1). A C1/C2 promise of volume, hold, texture, shaping/molding or shine goes to **Styling directly**, with no Leave-in check. Overlapping promises keep the full check (T15/D6/D7). Accepted consequences: Garnier Fructis „Leave-In Spray" Diamond Sleek ⇒ Leave-in; Wella EIMI Flowing Form ⇒ `provisional_boundary` | §4.2, §4.3 (ii), §4.5, §4.6 | Balea Föhnlotion Volume Effect and the 9 "care + no polymer" volume/hold/molding products ⇒ Styling directly |
| **PH2-2** | P-S2 | The PH1-16 hold covers **every Styling route the unchanged Leave-in letter would also claim** (all D6/D7 moves and owner-named routes). Held records get the **complete** Styling profile, marked `provisional`; they count toward "≥ 5 per subtype" (reported separately) and are **not matchable/recommendable** until the Leave-in addendum is approved | §4.5, §4.7, §4.8 | Balea Locken Revitalizing Spray: profiled as a `refresher` now, live only after the addendum |
| **PH2-3** | P-S3 | **D6 applies to any format**, not only sprays (format never routes) | §4.3 D6 | Balea Locken Crunchgel ⇒ Styling `gel` (held) |
| **PH2-4** | P-S4 | The heat check (PH1-15) reads Styling's own tail marker **T_S** (the Mask rule), the stricter side. `provides_heat_protection = true` needs a C1/C2 heat claim **and** an L9 member above T_S. LI `product_tested` without an L9 member stays `false` until ruled | §6.7 | John Frieda Ansatz-Booster: heat polymer after Parfum ⇒ `false` (claim still shown) |
| **PH2-5** | P-S5 | The buildup note is reused literally, **including limb 1**: permanently charged cationics, read internally from the LI §7.6 persistence enumeration only to feed the note | §6.4 | Alcina Styling Mousse (PQ-11) gets the note |
| **PH2-6** | P-S6 | Blind lanes get a **separate sealed S0 packet** (INCI, directions, confirmed name, C1/C2 promise and heat claims with tiers; no prior routes, no hold/finish/flexibility words). Each lane classifies F/U from the blind packet using its own S0 output | §10.2 | Balea Pro Styling Cream Traumlocken: the lane sees name and C2 promise for PH1-12/D7, never the dm „mittlerer Halt" attribute |

Also ruled on 2026-10-11 (not former proposals): **PH2-7** (dm „Halt" attribute on house-brand products = C2 `house_brand`, §2.2 A4, §7.2) · **PH2-8** (weight = the exact LI WT rule, §6.3) · **PH2-9** (T_S = the full Mask rule, §3.2) · **PH2-10** (Killer.Curls ⇒ `provisional_boundary (evidence_gate)`, §4.6) · **PH2-11** (housekeeping: Langhaarmädchen Lockenwunder ⇒ Leave-in; „ausbürstbar" stays a v0 convention; the charter's dropped P1 extras are tracked as open; the calibration file stays frozen, §13) · **PH2-12** (waxes, paraffin, microcrystalline wax and lanolin are rich members for Styling `weight`, §6.3, §13 item 15).

**Round-1 rulings (2026-10-12).** After blind round 1, Nick answered the four owner questions (R1-Q1 Henkel x/5 on every Henkel leave-on format except Taft gels; R1-Q2 anti-frizz on dry, finished hair = `shine_finish`; R1-Q3 care + curl definition ⇒ `mixed`, whatever the name; R1-Q4 `heat_activated` only on the product's own heat statement) and approved the technical bundle RG-1…RG-21 and P-1…P-8 as proposed (R1-T), with RG-5 adjusted to R1-Q3. The changelog under "Version" maps each to its section.

**Round-2 rulings (2026-10-12).** After blind round 2, Nick answered the four owner questions (R2-Q1 the care half is a promised care job and curl revival is refresh, narrowing R1-H2; R2-Q2 the water-based-serum exception reads the conditioner the D6 way; R2-Q3 blind S0 masking keeps job words and masks only grading words, refining PH2-6; R2-Q4 NEQI Diamond Glass All ⇒ Leave-in, extending D3; this historical outcome is superseded by R6-Q1) and approved the technical bundle RG2-4…RG2-27 and P2-1…P2-6 as proposed (R2-T), with RG2-16 ruled „open" and a targeted `smoothing_styler` capture before blind round 3. The v0.3 changelog under "Version" maps each to its section.

**Post-recheck clarifications (2026-10-12).** After the re-check capture (`capture/recheck-r6.json`), Nick ruled R2-H1…R2-H6: Wella EIMI Fresh Up's lines are C2 and promise no care job, and a manufacturer page's foreign INCI is a page error (R2-H1); GUHL 3in1 is a care product (R2-H2); Balea Haarwachs Styling Stick ⇒ `molding` (R2-H3); NEQI All's INCI is complete (R2-H4); feel lines never count as the care half (R2-H5); the claim host rule (R2-H6). They close §13.2 items 2–6; the amendment table under "Version" maps each to its section.

**Round-3 rulings (2026-10-12, folded in 2026-10-13).** After blind round 3, Nick ruled the lock reading (R3-L: „lanes agree" means resolved, not identical, plus a clean confirmation round), answered the four owner questions (R3-Q1 `smoothing_styler` may lock below 5 as a market-size exception; R3-Q2 the retailer title's product-type noun as the name fallback; R3-Q3 a one-job name wins over a care-joined lead line; R3-Q4 the D7 middle band extended to straightening/smoothing creams), ruled conflicting hold strengths to the middle level rounded up (R3-H, replacing PH1-8's outcome) and approved the technical bundle RG3-1…RG3-24 and P3-1…P3-7 (R3-T), with RG3-2 = R3-Q3, RG3-3 = R3-Q2, RG3-5 superseded by R3-Q4 and RG3-8 replaced by R3-H. The v0.4 changelog under "Version" maps each to its section; new open items are in §13.3.

**Round-4 rulings (2026-10-13).** After the confirmation round 4, Nick narrowed the lock scope (R4-L: only differences in user-facing fields block; calculation-log differences are fixed in the text without a further round), answered the seven owner questions (R4-Q1 a one-job C1/C2 name meets the promise input of the evidence gate; R4-Q2 „Beach Waves" is the salt-spray look and „Glaze"/„Topcoat"/„Gloss" are shine words; R4-Q3 only care verbs and care nouns are the care half; R4-Q4 the straightening limb covers fixative-led smoothing sprays with thin care; R4-Q5 registry D6/D7 rows keep ruling their records; R4-Q6 three confirmed text fixes; R4-Q7 a product noun names the job, a shine or strength qualifier is the finish or grade) and approved the remaining register RG4-1…RG4-27 and the packet fixes P4-1…P4-4 (R4-T). The v0.5 changelog under "Version" maps each to its section; new open items are in §13.4.

## 13. Ledger ambiguities and conflicts noticed (for the record)

Each item is marked with the ruling that resolved it, or stays marked **Open**.

### 13.1 Through Phase 2 (cited elsewhere as „§13 item n")

1. **PH1-16 vs PH1-11 coverage. Resolved in part — PH2-2.** Held records are profiled completely, counted toward "≥ 5 per subtype" and reported separately; committed counts per subtype are curl_cream 1, smoothing_styler 0, refresher 1, salt_spray 2, texture_spray 2, hair_powder 3 (§4.8, hand-annotated, to be re-derived in Phase 3). **Open:** 12 cohort records fail the S0 evidence gate (C3/C5 claims only); only more captures close that, and PH1-11's "≥ 5 per subtype" may still fall short on `smoothing_styler` (held 2). *Round 2 (computed, `round-2/diff.json` `coverage_per_subtype_agreed`):* 11 of 12 subtypes reach 5 when held records count; committed below 5: curl_cream 1, smoothing_styler 0, refresher 1; `smoothing_styler` has 1 agreed record (Color Wow, held). R2-T approved a targeted `smoothing_styler` capture before blind round 3 (§4.8). *Round 3 (computed, `round-3/diff.json`):* 10 of 12 subtypes reach 5 incl. held; short: `smoothing_styler` 2 (KMS 2-in-1 Spray committed, Color Wow held), `texture_spray` 4. **Resolved for `smoothing_styler` — R3-Q1** (market-size exception, §14.1); `texture_spray` is expected back above 5 under R3-Q3 and RG3-1/RG3-18 (§4.8), to be confirmed in round 4. **Resolved — round 4 (computed, `round-4/diff.json`):** 12 of 12 subtypes reach 5 when held records count (`texture_spray` 6 committed; `smoothing_styler` 1 committed + 4 held = 5, so R3-Q1's exception is not needed on that count); committed alone, 8 of 12 (short: curl_cream 1, smoothing_styler 1, salt_spray 4, refresher 1), which PH2-2 allows (§4.8). The evidence-gate count is a separate, open matter of captures (§13.2 item 1).
2. **Evidence gate vs owner-named examples. Resolved — PH2-10 for Killer.Curls.** Brilliantine and Color Wow (D2) and John Frieda Lockencreme Traumlocken (D7) have no C1/C2 promise (*since v0.5:* Brilliantine's C1 pack name meets input 1 under R4-Q1, R4-H (4); John Frieda carries a C1 line, §4.1 (d)); this standard routes them by their rulings (§4.1 owner-named routes). That override is limited to promise-evidence failures (no C1/C2 promise, a C1/C2 lead-promise conflict, a `mixed` reading); identity, INCI, directions and formula blocks are never lifted by a ruling, and a ruled route is never an F value (John Frieda's `hold_route` stays `ambiguous` under lexicon FX-2c). Kevin.Murphy Killer.Curls (C3 claims only) is `provisional_boundary (evidence_gate)` until a C1/C2 German claim is captured (PH2-10). **Released (RG-21):** the 2026-10 capture carries a C2 promise; D7 ⇒ styling `curl_cream`, held (PH2-2; §4.6).
3. **Routes the literal rules change versus the calibration file. Resolved — PH2-1.** `garnier-fructis-leave-in-spray-diamond-sleek-glaettungsspray` ⇒ `leave_in`; `wella-professionals-eimi-flowing-form` ⇒ `provisional_boundary (li_g0_provisional)` (*since R3-H2: held `smoothing_styler` via the D7 straightening limb, §4.6*). The calibration file has both as Styling `smoothing_styler`; it stays frozen and is re-derived in Phase 3 (PH2-11).
4. **PH1-1 wording. Resolved — PH2-9.** T_S is the full Mask rule (preservative, perfume, declared allergens, boosters in a pair, chelators), as approved under PH1-1. It decides the marker on 8 of 70 records (all unresolved via the third limb); no value moves.
5. **PH1-15 vs LI §13.3. Resolved in part — PH2-4.** The heat binary follows PH1-15's explicit text (C1/C2 claim **and** L9 pass, read against T_S); LI §13.3's claim-alone `true` is not used. **Open:** LI's `product_tested` state (claim + exact-product test, no L9 member) stays `false` until ruled.
6. **House-brand structured attributes. Resolved — PH2-7.** dm's „Halt" attribute on Balea/alverde products is C2 `house_brand` (LI rule 6, routes to review). Levels kept: Balea Ultra Volumen 4, Pro Traumlocken 2, MEN Pomade 2, Haarpuder 3, Volumenpuder 1. The round-2 G0 lane's C3 reading of the Traumlocken attribute is superseded.
7. **Leave-in WT rule vs the property-set dry run. Resolved — PH2-8.** Weight is the exact Leave-in WT rule including the LGN-pair route to `high`/`rich`; no extra anhydrous route (§6.3). Net cohort change: two curl creams move to `rich`; no other value moves.
8. **D6 wording "sprays". Resolved — PH2-3.** D6 applies to any format.
9. **Mixed-promise detection. Not a ruling item.** The round-2 G0 lane read Balea Pro Traumlocken as `mixed` from a definition claim, a softness claim and a heat bullet. §4.2 reads `mixed` for co-equal lead jobs **and, since R1-Q3, for any C1/C2 promise covering both care and curl definition**; either way Traumlocken reaches D7 first (PH1-12). The outcome is the same either way; the definition matters for future products.
10. **"Exactly one pair rule applies"** (charter §5 step 3) does not hold for products with a secondary heat claim and an aqueous formula (11 cohort records). §4.3 selects the pair by promise class in a fixed order. Not a ruling item.
11. **Booster-above-preservative limb.** Mask's third limb fires on 7 of 70 Styling records (common in L'Oréal and salon lists). It is part of the full Mask rule ruled in PH2-9, moves no value today (§3.3), but will keep producing `tail_marker_unresolved` reviews.
12. **Calibration-file labels to re-derive, not edit. PH2-11.** `calibration-set-r2.json` stays frozen: ruled outcomes are re-derived in the Phase 3 blind round, never patched. Known labels: `balea-locken-revitalizing-spray` `application_moment` (`second_day` ⇒ `dry_restyle`), the `claim_formula_conflict` flag on the same record (duration is not hold), the stale `proposed_finish` values (property set §9 item 4), and every route listed in item 3.
13. **Langhaarmädchen Lockenwunder. Resolved — PH2-11.** Leave-in by the PH1-13 R2 rule (cationic r4 above the preservative); no longer an unnamed edge case.
14. **„ausbürstbar". Resolved — PH2-11 (convention).** Stays a v0 convention: not a flexibility word. It is not a rule of the ledger beyond that housekeeping ruling.
15. **Resolved — PH2-12 (rich-member list for `weight`).** Waxes, paraffin, microcrystalline wax and lanolin are rich members for Styling (Styling-local; they move into the Leave-in addendum after lock).
16. **Open (tracked, not lock criteria) — charter P1 extras.** The charter's dropped P1 extras (≥ 80 % shelf coverage, every pair rule exercised at least once) are tracked as open, not as lock criteria (PH2-11).

### 13.2 Open after round 2 (2026-10-12)

Contradictions or gaps the ledger did not settle after round 2. Items 2–6 were settled by the post-recheck rulings R2-H1…R2-H5 (2026-10-12; marked below, the original text kept for the record); item 1 stays open and needs a capture or a blind-round test. The round-1 items closed by R1-H1…R1-H3 and the round-2 items closed by R2-Q1…R2-Q4 and R2-T are in the changelogs under "Version"; the v0.2 item on the tier of Taft Styling Balm Locken „Halt 3" is closed by the round-2 C1 capture („HALT 3", §7.3).

1. **Untested gaps carried from RG-20 (no rule added).** Round 2 exercised FX-U on a deciding case (`john-frieda-frizz-ease-3-tage-glatt-styling-spray`, Polysilicone-9: both lanes `hair_fixing_silicone_unresolved`), `target_zone: surface` on two finishing sticks and `hairline` on two edge gels (both lanes agree), and RG2-12/RG2-18/RG2-20 closed the word and rinse-out gaps. Still untested (node over both round-2 lanes): a salt-free `salt_spray` (every agreed `salt_spray` carries a `salt` mechanism in lane A); FX-2c on a record whose route turns on it (`john-frieda-frizz-ease-traumlocken-lockencreme` carries `acrylates_copolymer_function_unresolved`, but its route rests on the D7 naming); LI COND when T_LI is `none_visible` (no such record reached 3.4 (i), 3.4 (iii), D6 or D7); §5 rule 2's undefined „gel-type shaping product". Capture still queued: a German pack for `redken-styling-beach-spray` (no C1/C2 promise, directions a paraphrase). *Round 3 (node over lane A's round-3 outputs, 2026-10-13):* still untested — lane A emitted no `salt_spray` without a `salt` mechanism; FX-2c still decides no route (only `john-frieda-frizz-ease-traumlocken-lockencreme` carries `acrylates_copolymer_function_unresolved`, its route resting on the D7 naming); T_LI `none_visible` reached a border test once (`wella-eimi-smooth-thermal-image`, the D7-first check at the mixed stop, which fails on the sale limb without reading LI COND — and under RG3-4 the product no longer reaches that check). *Round 4 (node over both lanes' round-4 outputs, 2026-10-13):* still untested — every agreed `salt_spray` (6 in each lane) carries a `salt` mechanism, and R4-Q2's alverde Beach Waves (Maris Sal r2) is salted too; FX-2c still decides no route (both lanes carry `acrylates_copolymer_function_unresolved` only on `john-frieda-frizz-ease-traumlocken-lockencreme`, whose route the D7 row rules, §4.1 (d)); T_LI `none_visible` reached no LI COND read (29 lane-A records have it; the one on a border path, `syoss-glaze-topcoat`, stopped at the mixed stop, and under R4-Q2 it routes 3.5). `target_zone: surface` (2 finishing sticks) and a C1/C2 `fest` claim (2 got2b products) are exercised and agreed. Item 1 stays open for the salt-free salt spray, FX-2c on a deciding case and LI COND with T_LI `none_visible`.
2. **Resolved — R2-H4.** The re-check found the list complete (pack and neqi-hair.com agree, 14 items ending „Glycerin"); the truncation flag is withdrawn, so neither R2-Q4's block nor RG2-24 applies and the historical R2-Q4 (`leave_in`) stood without a block; R6-Q1 supersedes that route with held `smoothing_styler`. The general question (does a possibly truncated list end count as a tail defect) did not need an answer. *Original item:* **NEQI Diamond Glass All: R2-Q4's block vs the RG2-24 tail-defect rule.** R2-Q4: „It stays blocked until the possibly truncated dm INCI is re-checked (P2-3)." RG2-24 (approved by R2-T): „An INCI defect confined to the tail (at or below T_S: truncation inside the allergen block, a split fragrance/oil token) does not block S0; it carries review inci_tail_defect. A defect above T_S blocks." The record's flag reads „list_possibly_truncated: dm text field … ends 'Propylene glycol, Glycerin'"; its T_S is Ethylhexylglycerin r8 (unresolved; R2 marker Hydroxyacetophenone r9), so anything missing would follow r14. This file applies R2-Q4 (the specific ruling) and keeps the record blocked. Open: whether a possibly truncated list end counts as a defect „confined to the tail" for other products.
3. **Resolved — R2-H1.** Its promise lines are on wella.com/professional/de-DE (C2); „Detangling"/„entwirrend" is C3 retailer text only, so no C1/C2 care job is promised and the „Definition" lines cannot make it `mixed`; the border ingredient checks (D6/T15) decide in round 3 (§4.6, §11.14). The INCI printed on Wella's DE page is a page error (§2.2 A10). *Original item:* **Wella EIMI Nutricurls Fresh Up: R2-Q1's named outcome vs its own „Definition" line.** R2-Q1: „Wella Nutricurls Fresh Up ⇒ Leave-in by D6 (after its tier re-stamp, P2-6)" and „the definition half needs „definiert"/„Definition"". The record's name is „Curl Refreshing Detangling Spray" (a care job under R2-Q1), and its captured lines include „Frische deine Locken auf und erhalte Definition, Glanz und Anti-Frizz-Effekt." (typed `other`) and „Definition und Glanz" (typed `finish`). All its promise lines come from lookfantastic.de, re-stamped C3 by P2-6, so today it stops at the evidence gate. Open: whether a „Definition" line in a future C1/C2 capture makes it `mixed` (R1-Q3 / R2-Q1 letter) or the R2-Q1 example (`leave_in`) stands.
4. **Resolved — R2-H2.** GUHL 3in1 is a care product, not Styling; it stays in its current category (its leave-in curl-cream use is recorded for the Leave-in addendum). General reading: a care cream that can also be left in as a curl cream stays care; only a fixative-class polymer or the D7 middle band makes it Styling (§4.1). S0 needs no rinse-out exit: the record stays `provisional_boundary` in its current category. *Original item:* **GUHL 3in1: RG2-20 picks a rinse-out first-named use.** RG2-20 (approved by R2-T): „When directions offer leave-on and rinse-out uses, the first-named use decides the category; the other is recorded (PH1-7 one route)." `guhl-locken-pflege-und-styling-3in1` directions: „3 uses. Spülung: in nasse Längen, kurz einwirken, ausspülen. Kur: 3-5 min einwirken, ausspülen. Lockencreme: … (leave-in)". The first-named use is a rinse-out conditioner, which is none of the S0 routes (§4.0), while the registry keeps the product at `provisional_boundary (mixed_promise)` under R1-Q3 (§4.6). Both keep it in its current category under review; open: which reading the record carries and whether S0 needs a rinse-out exit.
5. **Resolved — R2-H3.** The name step (§4.2 step 1, RG2-21) precedes RG2-17; the stick (pack name „Styling WAX STICK") ⇒ `molding`; INCI from the pack (14 items) (§4.6, §11.16). *Original item:* **Balea Haarwachs Styling Stick: RG2-21 vs RG2-17.** RG2-21: „The product-type noun (Haarspray, Gel, Wachs, Puder, Schaumfestiger) names the job when it is the only job noun". RG2-17: „A lead line with no job word is skipped; the next C1/C2 line with a job word is the lead." The confirmed name „Haarwachs Styling Stick" has one product-type noun (Wachs), so step 1 could decide the lead from the name (a `molding` reading); RG2-17 was written for the same product (sealed slot 2), whose first line „Haarwachs-Stift für trendige Styles" it treats as job-less, so the lead becomes „Für ein glattes & glänzendes Finish" (`shine_finish`, both lanes). Open: whether a product-type noun in the name decides at step 1 when later C1/C2 lines name a more specific job.
6. **Resolved — R2-H5.** Feel lines never count as the care half (R2-Q1), incl. „Verbesserte Geschmeidigkeit"; Balea Pro Traumlocken keeps `curl_cream` via D7 before the mixed stop (PH1-12) (§11.2). *Original item:* **„Verbesserte Geschmeidigkeit" (Balea Pro Styling Cream Traumlocken, §11.2).** R2-Q1: „An adjective on the result („gepflegte Locken") or an ingredient/feel call-out does not count (SFS-15)." The RG2-1 proposed text approved with it says „It is not … a feel line („Verbesserte Geschmeidigkeit" stays care only where §11.2 says so)". §11.2 reads the line as part of a `mixed` promise. The route is the same either way (D7 fires before or at 3.4 (i)); open: whether §11.2's `mixed` reading stands as an exception or the class reads `styling_result`.

### 13.3 Open after round 3 (2026-10-13)

New contradictions or gaps the round-3 rulings did not settle. **All six are resolved by R3-H2 and R3-H4 (a)–(e) (2026-10-13)**, marked below; the original text is kept for the record.

1. **Resolved — R3-H2.** Flowing Form is covered by the D7 straightening extension ⇒ held `smoothing_styler`; R3-Q4's sentence „Wella Flowing Form ⇒ review" is withdrawn (§4.3 3.4 (i), §4.6). *Original item:* **R3-Q4's straightening sale vs Wella EIMI Flowing Form.** R3-Q4: a product with a fixative-class polymer above the tail and substantive conditioning, „sold as a straightening/smoothing styler („Straightening Creme", „Glättungscreme", lead „zur Glättung …")" ⇒ `smoothing_styler`; and „Products with a leading fixative and care but no straightening sale keep their current route (e.g. Wella Flowing Form ⇒ review)." The frozen record's C2 confirmed name is „EIMI Flowing Form (Smooth Flowing Form) Glättungscreme / Anti-Frizz Smoothing Balm" and its C2 lead is „ein glättender Haarbalsam, der luxuriöse Geschmeidigkeit mit flexibler Kontrolle schafft" (PVP r2, Polyquaternium-37 r3, Behentrimonium Chloride r5, all above T_LI Sodium Benzoate r7). Read literally, the extension's three limbs fire. This file follows the named outcome (review, §4.6). Open: what separates a straightening sale (KMS „zur Glättung von Locken mit Glätteisen oder Föhn") from a smoothing/anti-frizz balm sold as „Glättungscreme".
2. **Resolved — R3-H4 (a).** R3-H covers word-vs-word and number-vs-number conflicts; number-vs-word keeps „number beats word" (never empty either way), so Taft Styling Balm Locken stays 2 and Alcina Styling Mousse keeps its level (§7.2 steps 3 and 7). *Original item:* **R3-H vs §7.2 step 3 (number beats word).** R3-H covers „two strength statements on one source". §7.2 step 3 already settles a brand number against a word on the same pack or page (number wins: Taft Styling Balm Locken „Halt 3" ⇒ 2 against „Starker Halt" ⇒ 3, R1-Q1; Alcina Mousse Hold-Faktor 5/10 ⇒ 2 against „Starker" ⇒ 3). This file keeps step 3 and applies R3-H only to statements step 3 leaves in conflict (two words, as on Bali Flaxseed, or pack vs page). Open: whether R3-H was meant to replace step 3 for a number and a word on one source (it would move both examples to 3).
3. **Resolved — R3-H4 (b).** Three or more conflicting C1/C2 statements ⇒ the median level, rounded up (§7.2 step 7). *Original item:* **R3-H with three or more statements.** R3-H rules two conflicting statements („the middle level, rounded up"; 2 vs 4 ⇒ 3, 3 vs 4 ⇒ 4). A product with three C1/C2 statements at different levels (pack, page and a second page line) is not covered: the middle of the extremes and the median can differ (2, 2, 4 ⇒ 3 vs 2). No round-3 record shows the case. Open.
4. **Resolved — R3-H4 (c).** R3-L's „resolved, not identical" also governs criterion (c): every hold mismatch is resolved by a proven lane error or a closed rule gap (§14.1 (c)). *Original item:* **R3-L and lock criterion (c).** R3-L reads PH1-11's „lanes agree" (criterion b) as resolved, not identical. Criterion (c) — „two independent claim normalisations give identical hold levels" — is a separate sentence (round 3: 38 of 44 identical, node). Open: whether the resolved-not-identical reading also governs (c), or (c) still needs identical levels.
5. **Resolved — R3-H4 (d).** The fallback needs the product-type noun to agree across the retailer titles captured; if they differ ⇒ no fallback, review `retailer_title_type_noun_conflict` (§2.2 A3, §4.2). *Original item:* **R3-Q2 when retailer titles differ.** The fallback takes „the product-type noun of the retailer's product title". When two retailers title the same product with different type nouns (e.g. „Hitzeschutzspray" vs „Haarspray"), the ruling does not say which title counts; R1-H1 (two job words do not decide) covers two nouns in **one** name only. No round-3 record shows the case; the pack-name capture (P3-7) may make it moot. Open.
6. **Resolved — R3-H4 (e).** The D7 limbs read the fixative against T_S and conditioning by LI COND; a vacuous T_LI does not block them (KMS Straightening Creme, as R3-Q4 names it; §4.3 3.4 (i)). *Original item:* **D7 limbs with a vacuous T_LI.** D7 limb 1 reads „above T_LI", and LI §3.1.1 clause 6 says a vacuous marker cannot establish „present as architecture" by rank alone (the ordinal read decides). `kms-thermashape-straightening-creme` has a vacuous T_LI (Benzoic Acid r16, only Fragrance after it; E3-2); its limbs rest on the ordinal read and R3-Q4 names the outcome, so nothing moves. Open for future products: whether the D7 limbs (curl or straightening) may fire on an ordinal read when T_LI is vacuous, or the record goes to review.

### 13.4 After round 4 (2026-10-13) — all resolved by R4-H

**All five items are resolved by R4-H (2026-10-13)** (Claude housekeeping from the rulings' own logic, all calculation-log or already ruled); none is open. The original items are kept below for the record.

1. **Resolved — R4-H (1): no opaque `registry_row` pointer is added;** registry rows are matched by brand + pack name (R4-Q6, §4.6). *Original item:* **RG4-2's opaque `registry_row` pointer.** RG4-2's proposed fix has two parts: every registry row names brand and confirmed name (and GTIN), **and** the S0 packet of a registry product carries an opaque `registry_row` pointer. R4-Q6's ruling text names the first part (brand + pack name + old retailer title), which §4.6 now carries; the pointer is not built into the round-5 freeze (`r5/FREEZE-NOTES.md` §4). Open: whether the S0 packet should carry it (it would tell a lane that a ruling exists for the record).
2. **Resolved — R4-H (2): the Taft Gel Wax D8b flag is internal only (R4-L) and §7.5 is applied as written.** *Original item:* **D8b flag on Taft Gel Wax Glanz.** R4-Q7 keeps its D8b flag „internally". §7.5 rule 1 fires on a strength claim ≥ „stark", or on any hold claim on a `shine_finish`, `smoothing_styler` or `refresher`; as a `molding` with level 1 („Haltegrad 2", „langanhaltendem Halt") the letter does not fire. Open: whether rule 1 extends to any hold claim with no hold mechanism (it would add flags on other records), or this flag is a named exception. Internal flag only (R4-L).
3. **Resolved — R4-H (3): where a promise line has no recorded position, §4.2 step 2 reads packet order.** *Original item:* **Promise-line positions are mostly `unknown`.** P4-3 adds `position` to every promise line where the capture records it; the round-5 freeze finds a recorded position on 16 of the 453 promise lines (16 of the 350 C1/C2 lines in the S0 packets: 10 `pack_front`, 1 `headline`, 1 `bullet`, 4 `body`), and only 6 of the 31 records with a C1 promise line have a C1 `pack_front` line (node, `r5/FREEZE-NOTES.md` §6). On 2 of those 6 the first `pack_front` line differs from the first C1 line in packet order — `loreal-paris-elnett-stylingspray-hitzeschutz-3-tage-glatt` (pack front „Elnett de Luxe | POWER-SHINE-TECHNOLOGIE | 230°C HITZE STYLING-SPRAY …", the reading PH1-14 names, vs a heat-technology line first in packet order) and `guhl-locken-pflege-und-styling-3in1` („LOCKEN PFLEGE & STYLING" vs „Mit wenig Aufwand zu definierten Locken & Wellen"); both routes are ruled by their registry rows (PH1-14, R2-H2). §4.2 step 2 („the first C1 pack-front line") therefore falls back to the first C1 line in packet order wherever no C1 line carries `pack_front` — the reading lanes used in rounds 1–4, marked `[operational]` in §4.2. Open: whether packet order is accepted for step 2 or a position capture is needed before lock.
4. **Resolved — R4-H (4): Bumble and bumble „brilliantine" is a one-job C1 name under R4-Q1 (shine), consistent with its registry row** (§4.6 row, §4.1 (a), §11.8 updated; the round-6 freeze reads it `one_job`). *Original item:* **R4-Q1 and owner-named products with a C1/C2 name and no promise line.** `bumble-and-bumble-brilliantine` now carries the C1 pack name „brilliantine" (a shine product noun, D2/SD3); under R4-Q1 its name could meet §4.1 input 1 by itself, so its registry letter column („evidence gate (C3 only)") and its §4.1 (a) override may no longer apply. R4-Q1 does not name it, and the route (`shine_finish`, not held) is the same either way. Open: which reading the record carries (calculation log only).
5. **Resolved — R4-H (5), refined by R10-Q1: typed hold lines are secondary-job candidates under §6.10; RG3-16 strength/number evidence is STRONG, a bare hold mention WEAK.** *Original item:* **RG4-17 and typed hold lines.** RG4-17 orders the secondary candidates as „name job words first, then C1/C2 promise lines in packet order". RG-12 / RG3-16 make a C1/C2 **hold** line with a strength word or a brand number state `hold_fix`. Open: whether typed C1/C2 hold (and finish) lines are candidates after the promise lines in packet order, or only promise lines count. Where two promise-line candidates exist the result is the same (§11.2).

## 14. What this standard does not do, and what comes next

- It approves no product and authorises no catalog, Supabase, intake, matcher or copy change (charter §16). User-facing strings appear only where a ruling already fixed them (PH1-3, PH1-4 copy reused from Leave-in; „Hitzeschutz laut Hersteller", „Halt: keine Herstellerangabe", „Enthält Stylingpolymere" from PH1-15/D8/D8b).
- It sets no need tier, plan composition or ranking (SD4, D4, D5, D8 ranking are matching policy, charter §14).
- Rerun obligation: any change to a deterministic rule here or to a deterministic lexicon family re-derives every affected record (Mask rerun obligation); agreement statements never carry across a systemic change.

**Next (Phase 3, as planned before round 1; round 1 has run):** capture fills the evidence-gate gaps (Killer.Curls needs a C1/C2 German claim, PH2-10) and the short subtypes (charter §15 P1 (a)); re-derive the §4.8 counts and every route in §13 item 3 under PH2-1/PH2-2; freeze the S0 packet (PH2-6) and the F/U packets; blind calibration round 1 with two sealed lanes plus two claim normalisations; diff; owner rulings on disagreements; ≥ 6 unseen products with ≥ 2 boundary traps (candidates: a salt-free beach spray, a `target_zone: surface` product, a C1/C2 `fest` claim — the three ruled-but-uncalibrated values, property set §9 item 7); draft and queue the Leave-in addendum (PH1-16).

**Next after round 1 (v0.2):** the re-captures behind P-1…P-8 and RG-13 (dm attribute for Balea Ultra Volumen and Power Flex, full got2b pack name, Elnett 3 Tage Glatt real directions and pack-front line, A5 re-stamp of Langhaarmädchen, Glow & Care INCI separator and GTIN duplicate check, editor annotations out of claim text, Glow&Shine promise re-read, got2b and Taft Paste pack re-reads); re-freeze the S0 and F/U packets under v0.2; blind round 2 on every record a v0.2 change touches (rerun obligation); owner rulings on §13.2.

**Status of the round-2 next steps (2026-10-12):** §13.2 items 2–6 are settled by R2-H1…R2-H5 and the host question by R2-H6; the `smoothing_styler` capture (`capture/round3-smoothing-r5.json`, 4 products) and the re-check (`capture/recheck-r6.json`) are done; the round-3 freeze (`build/freeze-cohort-r3.js` ⇒ `r3/`) applies P2-1…P2-6 and the R2-H data. Remaining: blind round 3, owner rulings on what it finds, the Leave-in addendum.

**Status of the round-3 next steps (2026-10-13):** the pack-name and Elnett captures are done (`capture/packnames-r7.json`: 28 of 29 names confirmed at C1/C2, ISANA not found; Elnett directions ruled by R3-H3); §13.3 is settled by R3-H2…R3-H4; the round-4 freeze (`build/freeze-cohort-r4.js` ⇒ `r4/`) applies P3-1…P3-7 and the R3-H data. Remaining: the confirmation round 4 under R3-L, owner rulings on what it finds, the Leave-in addendum draft, the lock.

### 14.1 Lock criteria (PH1-11 as adjusted by PH1-16, R3-L, R3-Q1, R3-H4 (c) and R4-L)

**Current disposition `[LOCK-1-S]`: lock suspended (LOCK-1-S) — final re-check r11 pending. The earlier LOCK-1 receipt does not establish a current lock.**

Styling v1.0 is finished when:

- **(a) every subtype has ≥ 5 products**, each with a frozen packet; held records count and are reported separately (PH2-2). **Market-size exception `[R3-Q1]`:** `smoothing_styler` may lock below 5. Reason (owner): in the German market almost every smoothing product is sold care-, protection- or shine-first and routes elsewhere under the rules (the round-5 capture found 4 candidates); merging it into `blowdry_lotion` and further searching were considered and set aside. Counted: KMS Thermashape 2-in-1 Spray (committed) and Color Wow Dream Coat (held), agreed in round 3; Elnett Stylingspray 3 Tage Glatt (held; its C2 article directions are captured under R3-H3); KMS Thermashape Straightening Creme (held, R3-Q4) and Wella EIMI Flowing Form (held, R3-H2), each once round 4 confirms it. **Round 4:** all five agreed (1 committed + 4 held = 5), so the exception is no longer needed on the incl.-held count; Balea Glattspray Anti-Frizz (held, R4-Q4) would be a sixth once the final confirmation round (round 6; round 5 was aborted) confirms it.
- **(b) two sealed blind lanes agree on routes and properties — „agree" means resolved, not identical `[R3-L]`.** v1.0 may lock when every remaining A/B disagreement is resolved — either a lane error proven against a clear rule, or the rule gap behind it closed — **and** a final confirmation round shows only lane errors and no new rule gaps. Literal identity of both lanes on every value is not required. **Lock scope `[R4-L]`: only differences in user-facing fields block the lock** — `route` and recommendability (including held vs committed and the evidence gate), `styling_subtype`, `chaarlie_hold_level`, `weight`, `finish`, `flexibility`, `target_zone`, `application_moment`, `primary_job` and `secondary_jobs`, `provides_heat_protection`, and the user-shown notes (alcohol note, buildup note, neutral fact, „Hitzeschutz laut Hersteller", the hold display). **Differences confined to the calculation log do not block** — reason code, rule path, internal review triggers, internal confidence, internal flags (D8b, `claim_formula_conflict`, `name_from_retailer_title` …), lexicon placements that move no value: they are fixed in the text and documented, without a further round. Example (owner): `bali-curls-sleek-stick-brows-and-edges` — both round-4 lanes agree on every user field; only the reason code and rule path differ (RG4-26), so it does not block. Round 6 is the final confirmation round under this reading (round 5 was aborted before scoring).
- **(c) two independent claim normalisations agree on the hold levels — „agree" means resolved, not identical `[R3-H4 (c)]`.** Every hold-level mismatch between the two normalisations is resolved, either by a lane error proven against a clear rule or by a closed rule gap, and the confirmation round shows no new rule gap; literal identity on every level is not required (R3-L's reading, extended to this criterion; §13.3 item 4).
- **(d) ≥ 6 unseen products, ≥ 2 of them boundary traps, match pre-registered expectations** (met in round 2; round 3 had no unseen slots).
- **(e) a Leave-in addendum is drafted and queued** for right after the lock (PH1-16); it carries D6, D7 and the R3-Q4 straightening extension.

**Round-4 status (node, `round-4/diff.json` `lock_criteria`, read against the criteria above):** (a) **met** — 12 of 12 subtypes reach 5 incl. held, 8 of 12 committed; `smoothing_styler` 1 committed + 4 held (R3-Q1's exception not needed incl. held); (b-1) not met in round 4 — 80 core-field disagreements on 23 records (5 route splits): 9 lane errors (E4-1…E4-5), 71 cells behind 10 register items, every item ruled by R4-Q1…R4-Q7 / R4-T and folded into v0.5; (b-2) not met — round 4 raised 27 register items (15 causing a disagreement or needing an owner decision, 12 wording fixes on agreed outputs) and 4 packet issues, so round 5 is the final confirmation round, judged under R4-L; (c) **met** — 46 of 46 hold levels identical, all 6 round-3 mismatches resolved; (d) met in round 2; (e) **met** — the addendum draft is queued (`docs/research/leave-in-inci/v1.2/DRAFT-styling-border-addendum.md`; it must still take in R4-Q4 and the §4.1 (d) rows, §14).

**Round-3 status (node, `round-3/diff.json` `lock_criteria`, read against the criteria above):** (a) not met — `smoothing_styler` 2 (now under the R3-Q1 exception), `texture_spray` 4 (expected back above 5 under v0.4, §4.8); (b) 8 route and 138 core-field disagreements on 48 records, every one classified as a lane error (10, all lane B), a rule gap, a packet issue or an owner question and answered by the R3-* rulings — round 4 is the confirmation round R3-L requires; (c) 38 of 44 hold levels identical; (d) met in round 2; (e) no addendum draft yet.
